# MEKA OS build plan (the build board)

This file is the single source of truth for what is built, what is next and what is blocked. Unattended build runs
read it first and update it last. The owner reads it to see progress.

Status marks: `[x]` done · `[~]` in progress (partly landed) · `[ ]` not started · `[!]` blocked on the owner (see "Needs Meka").

## Rules every build run follows

1. **Hard constraints (never break, never "temporarily" relax):**
   - MEKA OS never holds trading or exchange credentials. Trading is event/approval based only.
   - Personal message sending is never more than "ask me, then send" (autonomy Level 3).
   - No unofficial WhatsApp client libraries. Messaging goes through the Android notification listener only.
   - Financial actions and trading approvals always need biometric confirmation.
   - Untrusted content (email, messages, web, news) never raises its own autonomy level (ADR-006).
2. **One item per run.** Take the first unchecked, unblocked item below. If it is too big for one run, land a
   working slice, mark it `[~]` and write what remains under the item.
3. **Main stays green.** Run `tools/core-verify.sh` and the relevant Gradle tests locally where possible, push, then
   watch CI. Pushes to `main` deploy the backend after CI passes. If CI fails, fix it in the same run or revert.
4. **Database migrations are additive only** (new tables/columns). Never drop or rewrite the owner's data.
5. **No new paid services, no new AWS resources that cost more than ~£2/month, no new third-party data sharing**
   without the owner's yes. Put the question under "Needs Meka" and skip to the next item.
6. **Animation is part of done.** Every new screen follows the motion catalogue below and honours reduced motion.
7. **Both apps.** A feature is done when it works on the Fold and the Mac (Mac may use a simpler layout).
8. **Dark is the default theme**; every screen must look right in Dark and Light.
9. **Commit messages** start with the milestone (`M1:`, `V1:`, `V2:`). Update this file in the same commit.
10. **Actions minutes are finite** (2,000 free standard minutes a month, then Meka's Actions budget, raised on
    2026-10-06 to about $40/month so the build can run around the clock; it stops at the limit). Docs-only commits
    don't trigger CI, and deploy only runs when a commit touches the server (backend/, infra/, core/, build files).
    **No daily push cap** (Meka removed it 2026-10-09 20:23: "happy with cost, just need to get this completed sooner";
    the GitHub budget limit still stops runs if it's ever reached). Keep one coherent slice per push. Runs go **back to back** (Meka, 2026-10-07): a run that landed
    something with CI green starts the next one with fire_trigger; the hourly schedule is the safety net. The `macos` job
    (about 10x the cost of Linux) runs weekly and on manual dispatch only, and build runs dispatch it **at most once a
    day, at night**: the first run at or after 21:00 UTC dispatches `ci` once if any commit since the last green
    dispatched/scheduled `ci` run touched `macos/`, `core/` or the build files. A Mac-touching item can be marked done
    before then ("Mac checked nightly"); if the nightly run is red, the next run fixes it first. If CI jobs sit queued
    with no runner, the allowance is used up: stop without pushing code; it resets on the 1st.
11. **Stop rule.** If something can't be undone and could reasonably go either way, don't do it: write it under
    "Needs Meka" and move on.

**Type weight (Meka, 2026-10-06):** calendar events and other context you don't act on use the regular `body` style;
the heavier `itemTitle` weight is for things you act on (tasks, habits, approvals). Meka found medium-weight event titles
too heavy on the Fold. **Fold review 2026-10-08:** task rows (Today, Needs you, the Calendar agenda) and booked-session rows
use `body` too; Up next's title is the one bold line.

## Motion catalogue (applies to every screen)

Tokens live in `design/tokens/tokens.json` (`motion`). Reduced motion = short cross-fade, no movement.

| Moment | Motion |
|---|---|
| App open | Greeting fades up, sections stagger in 40 ms apart, in the order they sit (Calm Today: Up next, then the timeline, then the moment cards — brief, review, shutdown, update — then Needs you, Anytime today and done today; core `TodayOrderRules`) |
| Theme change | Every colour blends across (`themeBlend`) |
| Complete a task | The accent ring sweeps once round from the top, the inside floods with the accent as it closes and the check strokes in, short leg first (`checkDraw`, 420 ms); then the row compresses and leaves; light haptic. Off: shown done at once |
| Up next changes | Card content cross-slides; old item settles into the list. Up next is the cover screen's card on every screen (UP NEXT, title, line, Done · Tomorrow · Open; chips press in with a light haptic). A late planned task: the label's colour blends to the accent and its line reads "Since 09:15" (its timeline row's line lit the same); Move to later presses in with a light haptic, the card cross-slides on and the undo bar rises ("Moved “…” to 15:30 · Undo"); the task's row glides to its new place. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Plan my day | Timeline blocks cascade in; Apply sends blocks into Today with a shared transition |
| Sync | Status dot breathes while syncing. Pull to sync (Today on the Fold): Today follows the finger past its top (rubber-banded), a brass ring fills with the pull and a tick haptic marks the point where letting go syncs; then the ring turns (one turn per 900 ms, at least one) while the content holds, a light haptic when done, and everything settles back on the expand spring. Mac: ⌘R / Sync Now / the command bar scale-fade the same ring in beside Search · Plan my day while it turns. Off: the ring fills and holds still |
| Switch section (shell) | Content slides an eighth of the width the way you moved (Mac: pushes along the sidebar); lit pill springs across; tick haptic; the tab icons (a sun on the horizon, "!" in a circle, a calendar, a speech bubble) morph outline → filled on the approve spring, the brass flood growing from half the shape while the icon swells to 1.15 and settles (the one going quiet empties the same way). Mac: the sidebar's SF Symbols swap to their filled variant with the system's replace transition and a bounce. Off: the fill cross-fades, no swell |
| Sheets and panes | Spring up from the bottom as a sheet (Dark: rounded top corners, a sliver of the screen above; Light: flush and square at the top, so no grey band shows under the status bar) while the screen behind dims to 50 % black (phone) / scale-fade (Mac, the system sheet); the dim fades out as the pane drops away; Fold unfold morphs list→two-pane. Reduced motion: cross-fades, the dim still fades |
| Habit tick | The ring sweeps round, fills and the check strokes in (`checkDraw`, like completing a task) as the circle pops with a spring (only when ticked on screen; already ticked shows done; unticking returns to the outline at once), streak number rolls; week dots fill; light haptic; an N-a-week habit's slot rings (one per go) fill in the accent on the complete spring instead of the daily dots (reduced motion: at once) |
| Fasting | Ring sweeps continuously (one-second linear glide); soft pulsing glow at goal; the 7 day bars fill on appear; light haptic on start/end; the history's heat strip fades in a week at a time, left to right, 40 ms apart, and History unfolds its rows in place; when an extended fast reaches its goal "You did it" pops in (spring) and the ring bursts once (light spreading out with twelve rays, light haptic). "Pick a day and time" unfolds in place (day chips, ‹ › steps the time by half hours with a tick haptic, the digits and the goal line cross-fade; Mac: a popover the system scale-fades). Reduced motion: the ring steps each second, steady glow, the strip shown at once, the line fades in with no burst |
| Goals | Progress bars fill on appear |
| Gym (booked sessions) | Today's session card rises into Today with the up-next group; as it moves on (booked → on now → "Did you go?" → went / rebooked) its content cross-slides; Went draws its check in (ring, fill, stroke) as it pops (spring) with a light haptic and the note field unfolds; Didn't go and Undo give a tick haptic. Goals: "Book my sessions" and Rotation chips blend (Mac: a switch and a menu); "Let MEKA book “gym”" presses in with a light haptic, folds away (the list's item motion) and its habit's row unfolds (Mac: the offer fades). Went / Didn't go on the "Did you go?" notification: the system's own motion as it turns into the quiet note. The session's row in Today's timeline glides into its place like an event's and leaves once it's over; on the "now" card (cover screen, Mac menu bar) the session cross-slides in like an event and Went / Didn't go press in (0.97) with a light / tick haptic. "Open Hevy ↗" on the card gives a light haptic and hands over to the app; in the habit's settings the line under the workout-app field cross-fades (saved, or "That isn't a web address" with a tick haptic). Reduced motion: cross-fades, no pop |
| Weekly review | Numbers count up; tiles and sections stagger in; goal bars fill on appear; ‹ › slide the week across the way you moved with a tick haptic (Mac: push, ⌘[ ⌘]); Done reviewing pops a check (spring) with a light haptic. The Sunday-evening card rises into Today like the brief's and opens Review with the shell's slide. Reduced motion: cross-fades, numbers shown at once |
| Approvals | Swipe to approve with spring; card slides away; fingerprint success morphs to a check; medium haptic |
| Needs you stack | The top card follows the finger and tilts; the move it leans to (Done · Tomorrow · Open) fades in and pops at the threshold with a tick haptic; letting go flies it off the way it went with a light haptic and the next card rises from the pile (two peek out behind); Open springs it back and the task opens; the undo bar rises. Mac: the same with a drag or ← → ↑. Reduced motion: no tilt or flight, cross-fades |
| Request cards (Needs you) | Cards from people Meka watches sit above the stack and stagger in after the after-work card; Add and Change press in (0.97) with a light haptic, Not a task with a tick; the card folds away (the list's item motion; Mac: expand spring) and the undo bar rises with what was done ("Added “Pick up dry cleaning” · Tomorrow", "Thu 15 Oct: work from home"); Change then springs Search up with the new task's detail over the results (Mac: the Search sheet scale-fades with it beside the results). Reduced motion: cross-fades "Straight away" / "In digest" beside each watched person in Work mode: the pill's colour blends across with a tick haptic (reduced motion: the same blend, no movement). |
| Message cards (Needs you) | Needs a reply and FYI cards (the messages assistant) sit above the request cards and stagger in after the after-work card; "Send all 3" fades in above them with the stagger when two or more short replies can go. Send, Open chat, Copy reply (Mac), Seen and Send all press in (0.97) with a light haptic, Not now and Edit with a tick; Edit unfolds the reply as a field in place (expand spring) and Done folds it back; an answered card folds away (the list's item motion; Mac: expand spring) and the undo bar rises with what was done ("Sent to Tunde", "Reply copied · paste it in WhatsApp", "Not now · Tunde"). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Group digest (Needs you) | From 12:30 and 18:30 the digest staggers in open at the top of Needs you ("Lunchtime digest · 3 groups · 64 messages"); in between it is one quiet line under the cards. Tapping the line or Show unfolds the group cards / a group's thread in place (expand spring) with a tick haptic; Hide folds it back. MEKA's gist fades in under a group's title once it is made. Caught up presses in with a light haptic, the cards fold away (the list's item motion) and the undo bar rises ("Barça lads · caught up · Undo"); Digest · Normal · Ignore switch with a tick and the undo bar ("Barça lads · back to WhatsApp as usual"). Mac: the digest's cards (title · count, the gist, who wrote) stagger in under the request cards with the head line; Caught up (one or all) presses in with a light haptic, the card folds away (expand spring) and the undo bar rises. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Messages setup (Work mode → Messages) | The section staggers in with Work mode's others; the status line cross-fades and its colour blends to the accent when something needs a look (no notification access, MEKA's AI off); "WhatsApp settings" unfolds its steps in place (expand spring; the chevron turns; tick haptic), open at first until notification access is on; "Keep from AI" / "Kept from AI" and a group's Digest · Normal · Ignore chips blend their colour with a tick haptic and the row's line cross-fades; a contact picked with "Keep someone else from the AI" gives a light haptic. Mac: a MESSAGES section (the line, then "Privacy, cost and limits" as a disclosure that unfolds on the expand spring). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Email triage | Items sort into their groups with a stagger |
| After-work summary | During work, "3 held for later" unfolds its preview in place (expand spring; the chevron turns; rows stagger in; tick haptic; Mac the same); the card sits above Needs you's stack; the pane springs up (Mac: sheet scale-fades); people sort into place with a stagger, urgent first; a person unfolds everything they sent in place (expand spring; Mac: tick haptic); Done gives a light haptic and the pane drops away. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Assistant | Ask's field asks MEKA with Return (Fold and Mac); the question shows, then a thinking shimmer (skeleton rows); the answer's lines fade in one after another (the stagger) and up to three cards rise under them; a card's button gives a light haptic, the card folds away (expand spring) and the undo bar rises with what it did ("Added “Milk” · Undo"); Undo brings the card back. The "MEKA's AI · On · $1.20 of $20 this month" line cross-fades, lit in the accent colour when it needs a look. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Talk to MEKA (voice orb) | The mic beside Ask's field (a still brass ring with a microphone) starts talking with a light haptic; the panel unfolds under the field (expand spring) with the orb: it breathes while thinking (the empty states' breath), swells up to 1.22× with Meka's voice while listening (level up fast, down slowly) with a brass fill, and sends three rippling rings out while MEKA speaks (1.4 s each); its line ("Listening…" · "Thinking…" · "Tap to interrupt") cross-fades; the live transcript types in under it; tapping the orb gives a tick haptic (interrupts while speaking, else ends) and the panel folds away; with "Talk over MEKA" on, Meka's voice rising over MEKA's does the same as the tap (no haptic: nothing was touched): the ripples stop, the line cross-fades to "Listening…" and the orb swells with his voice ("Talk or tap to interrupt" under it while MEKA speaks; Mac "Talk or click to interrupt"); a spoken yes folds the cards away and the undo bar rises as for a tap. Mac: the same orb beside the field (⌥Space starts it), clicking it interrupts or ends, the panel unfolds with the expand spring. A read-out ("read my brief", "the headlines", "Barça news") ripples like any answer while the line under the orb cross-fades to "Reading the headlines…" (Mac the same). Reduced motion: a still ring with a level bar, cross-fades |
| Capture | Field expands from the pill; captured item flies into the list. Capture sheet (share/widget/tile): springs up from the bottom, a check pops on Add and the sheet drops away; light haptic |
| Repeat and steps | Repeat picker unfolds from its row (Mac: menu); Skip/Tomorrow leave the list like a completion; a step tick draws its check the same way and pops with a spring, with a light haptic |
| Lists | Tab pill springs across (Mac: segmented control); a row unfolds its actions in place; Got it / Do it now leave like a completion with a light haptic; due chases and reviews are lit in the accent colour |
| Shopping (Lists → Shopping) | The fifth tab (the closed Fold reads "Waiting" for "Waiting for" so five fit); tapping a row's ring sweeps, fills and draws its check (`checkDraw`) with a light haptic and the row glides down under Got (the list's item motion; Mac: the replan animation); a got ring puts it back with a tick haptic; Remove and Clear take rows away with the list's item motion; the "3 to buy · 1 got" line cross-fades. Reduced motion: the check shown at once, cross-fades |
| Family page (Jeanette's shopping list, web) | Header, field and list fade up 40 ms apart; while loading, a skeleton shimmer; tapping a ring sweeps it round from the top, floods it with the accent and strokes the check in short leg first (`checkDraw`, 420 ms, with a pop) with a light vibration where the phone allows, then the row slides down and fades as it leaves for Got; a got ring puts it back with a tick vibration; new rows rise in 40 ms apart; the "3 to buy · 1 got" line cross-fades; anything tappable presses in to 0.97; nothing to buy shows the breathing brass ring. Reduced motion (the phone's setting): cross-fades, the check shown at once |
| Watch (Ask → More → Settings, both apps) | The row's title travels into the pane as it springs up (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); a skeleton shimmer until MEKA's server answers; the summary ("Galaxy Watch is linked · it shows Up next and takes Done") and each row's line cross-fade; rows stagger in 40 ms apart; Link (or Done/Return) gives a light haptic once the watch is linked, the field clears and its row staggers in; a refused code ("That code doesn't match a watch waiting to link…") unfolds in the accent (expand spring; Mac: opacity and a short drop) with a tick haptic; Unlink (critical colour) gives a tick haptic and the row leaves. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Galaxy Watch (the watch app) | Linking: "Link to MEKA", the code and its line cross-fade in (a new code cross-fades over the old); "9 min left" ticks down; when it runs out the code dims and "Tap for a new code" is a button. Once linked the link screen cross-fades into the day. The day: the now card (label, title, line, Done · Tomorrow or Went · Didn't go, "Then: …") cross-slides like the cover screen's when what's now changes (old out left a third of the width, new in from the right, on the replan spring with a fade); the fast's brass ring fills towards the goal on the appear spring (green once reached) with the time in it; every button presses in (0.97) on the complete spring, primary ones (Done, Went, Start fast) with the watch's confirm haptic, the others with a tick; the crown scrolls. Reduced motion (the watch's Remove animations): cross-fades, a press dims instead of scaling |
| Galaxy Watch tile and complication | None of their own: Wear OS draws the tile (swipe from the watch face) and the complication with the system's motion. The tile's Done (or Went) presses with the system's feedback and the tile is drawn again with what's next; a stale tile's tap does nothing. Reduced motion: unchanged |
| Galaxy Watch capture by voice | Capture presses in like the watch's other buttons (light tick); while listening the block cross-fades to "Listening…" in the accent with the words appearing as they're heard; once in, a confirm haptic and "Added “…”" cross-fades in with Undo for 5 s, then the button fades back (a failure's line for 4 s). On the tile, Capture sits beside Done as a quiet outlined pill (Wear OS draws the tile's motion). Reduced motion: the same cross-fades, which are already fades. The block is a polite live region, so a screen reader reads what was added. |
| Family (Ask → More → Settings, both apps) | The row's title travels into the pane as it springs up (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); a skeleton shimmer until MEKA's server answers; the summary ("Jeanette can see and add to the shopping list") and each link's line cross-fade, a waiting link's line and dot lit in the accent (joined: the success colour); rows stagger in 40 ms apart; a problem line ("Couldn't reach MEKA's server") unfolds in the accent (expand spring); "Make a link for Jeanette" presses in with a light haptic and hands over to the share sheet (Mac: "Link made" unfolds on the expand spring with Share… and Copy link, light haptic); Turn off (critical colour) gives a tick haptic and the row's line cross-fades to "Turned off". Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| School (Ask → More → Daily, both apps) | The row's title travels into the pane as it springs up (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); the summary ("Next day off: INSET day · Tue 27 Oct") and the line under the field ("Added …", or the "couldn't read" line lit in the accent) cross-fade; the Days off, Dates and Every week groups and their rows stagger in 40 ms apart and arrive, move and leave with the list's item motion (Mac: the expand spring); Add gives a light haptic, Remove a tick; a row's cover answer ("You're working from home Mon 27 Oct") is lit in the accent. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| School cover question (Needs you, both apps) | Above the requests: the card staggers in after the after-work card (and the triaged messages) and, once answered, folds away with the list's item motion (Mac: the expand spring) while the undo bar rises ("Working from home Mon 27 Oct" · Undo); I'll work from home (filled, the accent) gives a light haptic, Covered a tick; the Needs you badge and the command centre's heading count it. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| School day line (Today's header, the brief, the shutdown's tomorrow; both apps) | "Rex: PE today" sits under the weather (and the trains) in Today's header and cross-fades when it changes (a day ticking over, a line added); in the brief it appears with the title's stagger under the weather; the evening before, "Tomorrow · Rex: PE kit" appears with the shutdown's Tomorrow section in the accent. Nothing moves on its own; reduced motion: the short cross-fade. Each carries a spoken sentence ("School tomorrow: Rex needs PE kit.") for TalkBack/VoiceOver, and the read-aloud brief says it after the weather. |
| Evening shutdown (Done for the day) | Card rises into Today when the evening starts (work's end, else 20:00); sections stagger in, "Done today" first (the count rolls up, its rows with a brass check and the time stagger in, "+ 2 more" under them); a tick completes like a task (ring, check, light haptic) and the row leaves; carried rows leave the list and glide into Tomorrow while its summary rolls over; tomorrow's first commitment ("Tomorrow starts at 09:30 · Standup") staggers in under its label; Shut down pops a check (spring) with a light haptic and the pane drops away (Mac: sheet) while Today's watch face dims to night: the whole face blends to 55 % over `nightFall` (1.2 s) and its second hand and breath come to rest (redrawn each minute, the hands keeping time) until the next morning. Reduced motion: cross-fades, the dim a short cross-fade |
| Renewals | Like Lists: rows unfold their chips in place; "Renewed"/"Paid" on a repeating one slides its date line up to the next date (any list row's changed date does the same) and the row settles into its new place with a light haptic; a one-off leaves like a completion; what needs doing is lit in the accent colour. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| News ticker (Today) | Picture-and-headline cards drift right to left at a constant ~40 dp/s in a seamless loop, fading out at both ends; the match card leads in Barça's colour and its line cross-fades; a finger (Mac: hover) holds it, dragging scrubs; calm mode drifts twice each time Today appears then rests, and ▸ fades in to set it going again (tick haptic); a story springs the News pane up on it (Mac: the sheet scale-fades). Appearance → News ticker: chips blend (Mac: segmented control) and the line cross-fades. Reduced motion: no drift, one still card with ‹ › paging (tick haptic), cross-fades |
| Floating ticker (Mac, View menu) | The strip fades in over every app and drifts like Today's (always moving); hover holds it; the grip drags it and letting go springs it onto the nearer edge (top or bottom) with a light haptic; a story brings MEKA forward with the News sheet scale-fading onto it. Reduced motion: placed at once, one still card with ‹ › paging, fades only |
| News widget (Fold home screen) | The launcher flips the picture-and-headline cards every 5 s: the next card drifts in from the right and fades up as the old one drifts off left (the ticker's direction); it flips only while the home screen is showing; a tap brings MEKA up on Today with the News pane springing up on that story. Reduced motion (the phone's animations off): one still card, ‹ › page |
| News widget (Mac desktop) | None of its own: macOS desktop widgets can't animate; the system cross-fades it when MEKA writes new stories. Clicking a story brings MEKA forward with the News sheet scale-fading onto it |
| News (Ask → More → News) | The row's label travels into the title; Topics unfolds the chips in place, a chosen chip's colour blends with a tick haptic (Mac: Topics menu); lanes stagger in 40 ms apart; a story's detail springs up from the bottom (Mac: pushes across inside the sheet); Previous/Next (swipe sideways; Mac ← →) slide the story the way you moved with a tick haptic; the matchday line cross-fades as kick-off nears; from the command centre a story springs the News pane up over Today (Mac: the sheet scale-fades onto that story). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Morning brief | Card rises into Today when the morning starts (the end of quiet hours) and goes at noon or once read (Got it, or the pane open 5 s or more: Calm Today), folding away once the pane has dropped away (Fold: the list's item motion, the rows below gliding up; Mac: it shrinks to its top as it fades on the expand spring); sections stagger in; chases due today and what needs doing are lit in the accent colour; headlines stagger in last; Topics unfolds the topic chips in place, a chosen chip's colour blends across with a tick haptic (Mac: a Topics menu); Got it pops a check (spring) with a light haptic and the pane drops away (Mac: sheet). The card's line (weather · the day · the next match) cross-fades as it changes. ▶ Listen under the date presses in with a light haptic and its label cross-fades to "■ Stop" while MEKA reads (Stop: tick haptic); after the wake alarm it reads with no press. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Notifications | Sections stagger in; a chosen chip's colour blends across (Mac: segmented control and menus); quiet-hour times unfold when turned on; the "what happens next" lines cross-fade as settings change. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Search | The pane springs up with the field focused (Mac: ⌘F sheet); groups stagger in 40 ms apart and rows glide as results narrow while typing; an open task's detail springs up over the results (Mac: pushes across beside them); a done task unfolds Reopen in place; a list item, goal or habit opens its tab with the shell's slide and its row unfolded. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Today timeline | Rows glide to their new places as the day moves on (the quiet Work band too, which leaves once work is over) (a finished event slips into "3 earlier", the now line moves down); "3 earlier" unfolds the finished events in place with its chevron turning; the now line's dot breathes gently and the free time from now sits on it ("1 h free until Work"), cross-fading as it changes; the Up next event line fades in an hour before. Reduced motion: cross-fades, steady dot |
| Event detail | Tapping an event (a row, an all-day chip, Up next's event line) springs its pane up from the bottom (Mac: sheet scale-fades); sections stagger in 40 ms apart; Join gives a light haptic; the status line ("In 25 min") cross-fades as time moves on. Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Calendar | Day pills slide between weeks (swipe on the Fold; ‹ › push on the Mac); the lit pill's colour blends across with a tick haptic; tapping a day springs the agenda to it (the expand spring; from far away it jumps to a few rows short and springs the rest); agenda sections stagger in 40 ms apart and rows glide as the day moves on; a day's "+2 more" unfolds its other all-day rows with the list's item motion (Mac: expand spring); the calendar key fades in with the summary; the now line's dot breathes. Reduced motion: jumps and week changes at once, cross-fades, steady dot |
| App update | The "MEKA update ready" card rises into Today like the brief's; its line cross-fades between states; Install gives a light haptic and the bar fills as the build downloads; then Android's own Install prompt. Mac: the publish sheet scale-fades, a shimmer runs while it uploads, Published pops a check (spring) with a light haptic. Reduced motion: the bar jumps, cross-fades |
| Export (your data) | Sections stagger in; a shimmer runs while the file is built; once saved a check pops (spring) with a light haptic and the file's name; the pane springs up from Today's header (Mac: sheet scale-fades). Reduced motion: cross-fades |
| Talk (Ask → More) | The row's title travels into the pane as it springs up (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); sections stagger in 40 ms apart (Side button · Headphones · Safety; Mac: On the Mac · Safety); the side button's status line blends to the accent once MEKA is the digital assistant (re-read when MEKA comes back from Settings); Open default apps presses in with a tick haptic. The mic on the bedside clock and at the end of Today's capture bar (Mac: the capture field's orb, press style) presses in (0.97) with a light haptic (dimmed with quiet hours at the bedside); at the bedside the Talk pane springs up over the clock with the orb listening and drops away on ‹ Clock; from the capture bar Ask opens with the shell's slide. Holding the side key or the headphones' button, tapping the Talk widget, or opening MEKA from the launcher with Bluetooth audio or in the car opens Ask with the shell's slide and the Talk panel unfolding as for the mic (light haptic). The Talk widget itself: the launcher's own motion. "When I open MEKA": Turn on / Turn off presses in with a tick haptic and the status line blends to the accent while on; with it on, a launcher open slides to Ask, a quiet chime, and the Talk panel unfolds listening; "Too noisy — tap to talk" fades in under the resting orb (accent) instead when the room is loud; nothing said in 6 s and the panel folds away and Today slides back. | Setup card (Today) | Under the health line (not on the cover screen): "SET UP MEKA" and "3 steps left · Outlook calendar, Calendar editing, Pick a voice" rise with Today's stagger; the line cross-fades as steps are done; Open Setup (light haptic) slides to Ask with the Setup pane springing up (Mac: the sheet scale-fades); Not today (tick haptic) folds the card away on the expand spring until tomorrow; it goes the same way once everything is set. Reduced motion: fades |
| Loading | Skeleton shimmer, never a spinner on its own |

- [x] Hands-free phone updates (Meka approved 2026-10-07 23:09): after every green CI run on main that touched `android/` or `core/`, GitHub builds the debug APK and publishes it, so the Fold offers "MEKA update ready" with no Mac involved. **Signing:** the Mac's debug keystore is in the GitHub Actions secret `MEKA_FOLD_SIGNING_KEY` (base64; Meka adds it with "Copy phone signing key.command"; password/alias are the debug defaults `android`/`androiddebugkey`); the workflow decodes it to a temp file, signs, and deletes it; never echoed or uploaded as an artifact. If the secret is missing the job skips with a notice (not a failure). versionCode stays the commit count, so CI and Mac builds never clash (same commit = same build; the server already refuses a build that isn't newer). **Publishing identity (security):** a dedicated release-only publisher — not a household device: a P-256 key whose private half lives in Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/release-publisher`, readable only by the GitHub OIDC deploy role; its public key registered on the server as kind `release_publisher` for Meka's household, which may call only `/v1/releases/upload` and `/v1/releases/chunk` (every other route 403; unit-test that), and is listed in Activity as "GitHub build". The phone still checks the SHA-256 and Android still refuses anything not signed with the installed key; nothing installs without Meka's tap. Add a quiet "MEKA update ready" notification (low-importance channel, at most once per build) so he knows without opening the app. Keep `publish-fold.sh` working as the manual fallback. Infra test for the secret and role grant; backend tests for the publisher scope. Then tell Meka in the run summary that phone updates arrive on their own.
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-07 (core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* the pipeline. **Infra:** Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/release-publisher` (the P-256 private key; a resource-policy Deny on GetSecretValue for every principal except `role/meka-os-github-deploy`, the service included) and `meka-os-dev/release-publisher-public` (`{"public_key": …}`, read by the service; env `MEKA_RELEASE_PUBLISHER_SECRET`), both `{}` until the workflow makes the pair (about $0.80/month together); infra test for the deny, no grant of the private key and the service's read. **Server:** `ReleasePublisher` (backend/ReleasePublisher.kt): not a household device and no bearer secret — `Authorization: Publisher github-build` plus the usual request signature, checked against the public key (re-read each minute); it publishes for the server's one household (refused if there are none or several) and may call only `/v1/releases/latest` (to see what is already published, which the plan's "chunk" became: downloading builds isn't the publisher's business) and `/v1/releases/upload`; every other route answers 403, no device can be called `github-build` (device ids are `[a-z0-9]`), and its builds are stored as published by `github-build`. Backend tests (ReleasePublisherTest: publish → the Fold fetches it, same build again, not newer, every other route 403 incl. chunk, another key / replay / wrong name / wrong route, no key set, two households). The same file has the publisher's client and commands (`backend publisher-keygen | publisher-latest | publisher-publish`, no database; the private key only from the environment). **Workflow:** a `publish-fold` job in deploy.yml after the server deploy (or its skip): skips with a notice when `MEKA_FOLD_SIGNING_KEY` isn't set; makes the publisher's key pair the first time (public half first); asks the server for the published build and skips with a notice unless `android/`, `core/`, `design/` or the build files changed since the commit that build was made from (main is linear: build N is the Nth commit); decodes the keystore into the runner's debug-key place, checks it opens with the debug defaults, builds `assembleDebug` (full history, so versionCode is the commit count), checks with apksigner that the APK carries that very certificate, publishes, and always deletes both keys from the runner. "Not newer" (e.g. the Mac published this commit first) is a notice, not a failure. **Fold:** a quiet "MEKA update ready" notification (`app_update` channel, low importance, "Build 412 · 24.3 MB · open MEKA to install") once per build and only while MEKA isn't on screen; Later or nothing on offer withdraws it; `AppUpdateRules.shouldNotify/told/notificationLine`, unit-tested. **Mac:** `tools/Copy phone signing key.command` copies its debug key as base64 and opens the repo's new-secret page (Needs Meka #14). `publish-fold.sh` is unchanged as the manual fallback.
  - *Live 2026-10-07:* CI green (run 37697302079); deploy run 37697539793 deployed the server, made the publisher's key pair and **published the first phone build from GitHub** (the signing secret was already set, and the APK's certificate matched the Mac's debug key).
  - *Slice 2, 2026-10-07 (core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* the GitHub build in Activity. When a build from the release-only publisher is complete, the server (`ReleaseActivity`, backend/ReleasePublisher.kt, through `Releases`' new `onPublished`) writes one `agent_action` entry of the new kind `PUBLISHED` into Meka's synced data as server-authored ops (ADR-008 addendum; fixed op ids, so once per build) and wakes the devices: "GitHub build published build 412" · "MEKA 0.1.412 · 24.3 MB · install it from Today on the Fold" · "Why: Hands-free phone updates · after a green CI run on main", nothing to undo, and the week line counts "1 phone build". Both apps' Activity panes show it as they show every entry (no app change); builds the Mac publishes by hand aren't listed. Core `ActivityRules.releaseId/releaseSummary/releaseFields`; entries of a kind an app doesn't know are skipped. Tests: ActivityTest (both devices, unknown kind), backend ReleaseActivityTest (listed once, re-publish writes nothing, next build a second entry, a Mac build or an incomplete one isn't listed, wake once). CI green (run 37699788994; macOS job at the next nightly run; the server redeploys).

- [x] Living Today — an always-on signature animation (Meka, 2026-10-08 21:15: "some work but too quick or subtle… I'd like a constant animation every time I open the app… you decide, don't make it look cheap"). Decided: a luxury-watch feel, built around the **Day ring**, gold on black; one ambient element, never several competing.
  1. **The Day ring is always on Today** (not only as an opening moment, and still after Shut down — then it shows tomorrow's first commitment): pinned under the greeting at a comfortable size (≈ 180 dp closed Fold, larger unfolded; Mac at the top of Today).
  2. **A sweeping gold second hand**: a fine brass hand with a soft comet tail orbits the ring once a minute, **smooth sweep (like a mechanical watch), not ticking**; on each new minute the "now" mark nudges forward with a tiny spring.
  3. **Breathing glow**: the ring's brass edge brightens and dims slowly (≈ 5 s cycle, 60 %→100 %), like a sleeping laptop light — calm, not flashing. At the top of each hour a single gentle shimmer runs round the ring.
  4. **The ring carries the day**: today's events are brass arcs on the ring (work hours as a faint band, gym/training highlighted), the current one glowing; tapping an arc opens the event; a running fast shows as an inner arc filling.
  5. **Every open is a moment, not once a day**: the ring draws itself in (≈ 900 ms Expressive, 500 ms Subtle) and the cards rise in a stagger each time Today comes to the front; the long first-of-day version adds the hour marks fading in one by one and the greeting letters lifting in.
  6. **Expressive must be obvious**: Expressive durations ×1.4 and larger travel (cards rise 24 dp, not 8 dp); Subtle = today's values; Off = still ring with no hand.
  7. Same on the bedside clock (larger ring, slower glow) and a mini ring on the cover screen. Mac: the same ring at the top of Today.
  8. Battery/quality: drawn on the GPU (Canvas, no recomposition per frame — `graphicsLayer` rotation, `drawWithCache`), 60 fps only while visible, paused when off screen or in power saving (then the hand jumps once a minute). Not added: coin flips, sparkles, bouncing icons (read cheap).
  Take right after the "Can't see the animations" item; verify on the emulator by sampling the hand angle and glow alpha across frames.

Weather nudges through the governor at Digest by default ("Rain at 17:30 — Training at SG18"). Unit-test parsing on saved samples.

- [x] Talk without tapping the mic (Meka, 2026-10-08 21:59: tapping the mic doesn't feel natural; wants to open MEKA and talk; worried about noisy places). Decided:
  1. **Side button = "open MEKA already listening"** (default digital assistant role, `ROLE_ASSISTANT` / `VoiceInteractionService` or the assist intent): long-press the side key → MEKA's Talk panel opens listening. The setup screen walks Meka through Settings → Apps → Default apps → Digital assistant app → MEKA (Samsung: Side key → Press and hold → Wake Bixby→ change to "Digital assistant").
  2. **Auto-listen where it's clearly wanted:** when MEKA opens with **Bluetooth headphones or a car** connected, from the **home-screen Talk widget**, from the bedside clock (half folded), or from the cover screen's mic.
  3. **"Listen when I open MEKA" — a setting, off by default** (Ask → More → Talk). When on: the orb appears and listens for up to 6 s after Today opens; stops on silence; **skips when it's noisy** (measures the room for 300 ms first; above ~60 dBA it shows "Too noisy — tap to talk" instead); never when opened from a notification; a quiet chime marks listening.
  4. **Safety unchanged:** nothing destructive or sent without a spoken/tapped yes; every change has Undo; other people's speech reads as Meka's, so the confirmation is the real guard — speaker recognition is not built now (no reliable public on-device speaker-ID API, poor in noise; revisit in V2 as an optional extra check).
  5. **"Hey MEKA" wake word** stays a later option (battery, false triggers).

- [~] **Nightly Mac build red (take first).** Dispatched `ci` run 37847660797 (2026-10-08 21:33 UTC, main at b75a901): core, android and tokens-and-infra green; the `macos` job (113552473230) fails to compile: `macos/MekaOS/CoreModel.swift:1444: type 'EventUndoOffer.Action' does not conform to protocol 'Equatable'` — the `case talk([AskUndo], [Int])` added in b7bfad2 (Talk to MEKA slice 3) carries `[any AskUndo]` (a Kotlin sealed interface, so a Swift protocol, not Equatable), which blocks the synthesised `==`. Fix: write `Action`'s `==` by hand (every other case as now; `talk` equal when the Ints match and the undos are the same objects, `zip` + `(a as AnyObject) === (b as AnyObject)`), or drop `Equatable` from `Action` if nothing compares it (check `EventUndoOffer`'s own `==` and the Mac tests). The push cap (70) was reached today, so this waits for the next UTC day; then push it, watch CI, and dispatch `ci` once to prove the macOS job green (it's the only failure; the job's log is readable with get_job_logs).
  - *Fix, 2026-10-09:* the job's full log (get_job_logs) shows this is the only compile error. `EventUndoOffer.Action` is no longer `Equatable` (nothing compares actions; the undo button switches on it), and `EventUndoOffer`'s `==` is written by hand: the same offer (its id and message), which is all the undo bar's `.animation(value:)` needs. Mac test `EventUndoOfferTests`. Dispatched `ci` run 37864821263 (core, android, tokens-and-infra green): the Equatable error is gone, and the compile then reached three strict-concurrency errors in `CoreModel.swift` — `sending 'history' / 'cards' / 'undos' risks causing data races` for Talk's `core.talk(history:)`, `core.doTalk(cards:)` and `undoTalk(undos:)`: an *array* of Kotlin objects is a stdlib type, so `@preconcurrency import MekaKit` doesn't quiet it the way it does a single Kotlin object. Each now crosses once through a `nonisolated(unsafe) let` copy (Kotlin values are immutable). That was the whole error list in the job's log. **Mac checked nightly:** today's one dispatch is spent, so the 21:00 UTC run proves the macOS job; if it is still red, fixing it comes first. Rule for Swift from here: never hand a `[KotlinType]` from the main actor to a core call directly.
  - *Nightly 2026-10-09 (dispatched `ci` run 37990720470, main at 156cb2c):* android and tokens-and-infra green; core-and-backend never started (Docker Hub's pull rate limit on `postgres:17` in "Initialize containers"; the same tree was green in push run 37988210481). The `macos` job (114023879093) got past the Kotlin/Native tests and the XCFramework, and the earlier Talk errors are gone. The whole log (get_job_logs works for this job) has one compile error: `MekaSpeaker.swift:282: capture of 'utterance' with non-Sendable type 'AVSpeechUtterance' in a '@Sendable' closure` (the device-voice slice's `SpeechDone`, which looked up `ObjectIdentifier(utterance)` inside `OSAllocatedUnfairLock.withLock`). **Fix:** the identity is taken outside the closure, so only the Sendable `ObjectIdentifier` crosses. **Mac checked nightly:** tonight's dispatch is spent, so the next run at or after 21:00 UTC on 2026-10-10 proves the macOS job (its Swift tests have not been reached since 2026-10-08). If it is still red, fixing it comes first. Rule for Swift from here: never capture an AVFoundation/AppKit object in a `withLock` or `@Sendable` closure; capture its `ObjectIdentifier` or a value copy.
  - *CI, 2026-10-09 21:19:* the push run for that fix (37992675562) hit the same Docker Hub pull limit on `postgres:17` before any test ran (android and tokens-and-infra green; the change is Swift only). The image now builds with the engine's own builder (`docker build --platform linux/arm64` + `docker push`, no buildx container; no RUN steps, so no emulation) on `public.ecr.aws/docker/library/eclipse-temurin:21-jre`, so neither CI nor the deploy touches Docker Hub. - [x] Requests from my wife become tasks (Meka, 2026-10-09 09:21: "if my wife sends something like a task, or asks me to work from home on X date, and asks me to do something… this is the thing I keep forgetting"). Approved by Meka (he asked for it; the AI reads messages only from people he chooses). Build:
  1. **"Watch for requests from"** list in Work mode → People (default: the Family list; Meka adds his wife). Applies **all day**, not only at work. Official notification access only (WhatsApp, SMS/RCS, Samsung Messages; no WhatsApp libraries); 1:1 chats only by default (a switch for named groups).
  2. On each new message from a watched person, the Fold sends **only that message's text** (plus sender label and time; never contacts, numbers or other chats) to the AI layer with MEKA's tools schema; the AI returns zero or more **proposals**: task ("Pick up dry cleaning", due "tomorrow"), dated commitment ("Work from home Thu 15 Oct" → a Work-mode exception + calendar block), event ("Parents' evening Tue 18:00"), reminder ("Call your mum at 7"), or nothing. Untrusted content (ADR-006): never acted on automatically.
  3. **Needs you card**: "From Wife · 14:02 — 'can you pick up the dry cleaning tomorrow?'" → **Add task: Pick up dry cleaning · tomorrow** [Add] [Change] [Not a task]; dates resolved against Meka's calendar and work days; WFH requests propose switching that day's work mode to "Home" and blocking the day; duplicates merged (same request repeated). Quiet heads-up notification at Digest by default; a switch for "notify straight away" for the wife.
  4. **Never replies**, never marks read. Optional later: a one-tap "Done ✓ — reply 'Will do'" draft for approval (personal messages stay at "ask me").
  5. Limits shown in the screen honestly: only what Android's notification shows (very long messages may be cut; voice notes and photos can't be read — MEKA says "Wife sent a voice note" and offers a reminder to listen); messages read first on another device may not notify.
  6. Cost: one small AI call per message from watched people (pennies a month). Unit-test the proposal parsing and date resolution ("Thursday", "next Friday", "on the 15th") in core; Fold UI test for the card.
  Take right after the current Fold review items.
  - *Slice 1 done 2026-10-09 (core + server, no UI yet; core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* core `MessageRequestRules` (MessageRequests.kt; `MessageRequestsTest`): `shouldRead` (a message with text from the family list or the watch list, 1:1 unless its group is named), `worthAsking` / `isVoiceNote` / `voiceNoteProposal` (voice notes and bare photos never go to the AI; a voice note becomes "Listen to Wife's voice note"), `check` (known kinds only, a clean title, the day resolved again from the message's own when-words, never past or over a year ahead, WFH and events need a day, duplicates merged), `cards` (the Needs you card: "From Wife · 14:02", the quote, "Add task: Pick up dry cleaning · Tomorrow", "Work from home · Thu 15 Oct" with "Sets Thu 15 Oct to Home and blocks the day", Add · Change · Not a task, a spoken line; the same request again from the same person, or a re-post, makes no second card); `RequestDates.resolve` ("tomorrow", "Thursday", "next Friday", "on the 15th", "15 Oct", "Tue at 6pm"; Add event's reading plus "the 15th"). Wire `MessageRequestCodec` (`MessageRequestCodecTest`). Server `MessageRequestService` + `POST /v1/ai/message-request` (keyed devices; one tool-less SMALL call under `extract.message`, the message in a data block it can't close; nothing kept; `AiMessageRequestsTest`). ADR-006 addendum. Commit 87699e6, CI run 37913083380 green (first try).

Core `RequestCards` (RequestCards.kt; `RequestCardsTest`): new additive entity `request_card` (ADR-008 addendum; `resolved` TrueWins, the rest LWW, written once by the Fold) — `save(message, proposals)` (no second card for a re-post, for the same request from the same person while one is open, or for a card already answered), `open()` (oldest first, rebuilt each read so "Tomorrow" becomes "Today"; gone after 7 days or once its day has passed), `find`, `resolve(id, added · declined · changed)` (clears it on both devices and blanks the text); `MessageRequestRules.card` (one card, shared by `cards` and the store). Facade: `MekaCore.readRequest(item, lists, watching, groups)` → `RequestRead.Skipped` · `Read(newCards)` · `Unavailable(line)`: only a message `shouldRead` allows; a voice note becomes "Listen to Wife's voice note" with no AI; otherwise one message (text, sender's label, its time, today's date and Meka's work days) to `AiApi.messageRequest` (`POST /v1/ai/message-request`, signed; a server without it is "off"), the proposals checked against the day the message came, saved as cards; `MekaCore.requests` (StateFlow of the open cards) and `declineRequest(id)`. - [x] **Brief changes voice mid-read (Meka, 2026-10-10 07:50: "some of it sounded natural and some didn't — when it read the matches bit it sounded robotic").** Likely cause: in a long reading a piece that misses `NEXT_AUDIO_MS` (6 s) goes to the device voice (`SpeechRules.onMiss` → PIECE_ON_DEVICE); the matches section is long and generative Polly is slowest on long pieces. Fix: (1) **confirm first** from the timing line / server log which pieces were late or failed (and that nothing in the match text — "U7", "v", "–", score dashes, club abbreviations — makes Polly fail); (2) **fetch ahead**: when Listen starts, request every piece of the brief in order (2–3 in flight) while the first plays, so later pieces are ready before they are needed; (3) **never switch voice in a reading** while the server is answering: a late piece waits (MEKA's own short pause, no "One moment…" mid-brief) up to ~15 s, and only a refused/failed server hands that piece to the device; (4) **say matches the way people do**: "U7" → "under-sevens", "v" → "versus", "BUFC" → "Biggleswade United" (club abbreviations from the calendar), times as "ten o'clock", and name the child: **U7 fixtures are Rex's, U10 fixtures are Logan's** (Meka, 2026-10-10) — "Rex's under-sevens play Arlesey at ten"; (5) cap a reading piece at ~200 characters so each clip is quick. Tests: BriefSpeechTest (match wording, child names), SpeechTest (reading never falls back while the server answers). Also use the children's names in the weekend-football surfaces (kit reminder "Pack Rex's kit…", running-late draft, How did it go) where the team is U7/U10.

The football surfaces name the child: kit task "Pack Rex's kit for BUFC U7s v Arlesey", running late "Hi, sorry, Rex and I are running about 10 min late for …", the prompt "How did Rex's match go?" with the fixture in its line (other age groups unchanged). **Fold** and **Mac** `MekaSpeaker.speakNow` keep a queue of up to `ahead` pieces in flight (conversations unchanged: one ahead, the hold as before). Group chatter without a mention → **Group digest**.
  2. **Needs a reply → drafted reply for approval** in Needs you: "Tunde: are you coming Saturday?" → draft "Yes, I'll be there — what time?" [Send] [Edit] [Not now]. Sending uses Android's own notification **Reply** action (RemoteInput — official, no WhatsApp libraries) while the notification is live; otherwise "Open chat" via WhatsApp's official click-to-chat link with the draft copied, Meka taps send. **Personal messages stay at "ask me": nothing is ever sent without Meka's tap**; never to family without his tap either; easy replies may be batch-approved ("Send all 3") like email.
  3. **Action for you → task proposal** (as in the item above: tasks, dated commitments, WFH days, events), with the sender and quote.

MEKA clears the group's notifications after capturing them (`cancelNotification`) so groups stop buzzing; a per-group switch: Digest · Normal · Ignore.
  5. Work mode keeps its after-work summary; this replaces nothing, it adds triage all day.
  6. Privacy and cost shown in the screen: only message text + sender label go to the AI, one batched call per few minutes for groups, per message for 1:1; estimated a few pounds a month at most within the $40 cap; per-person/per-group "never send to AI" switch. Nothing stored on the server beyond the proposals; raw messages stay Keystore-sealed on the phone, dropped after 7 days.
  7. Limits stated: only what notifications show (long texts cut, voice notes/photos not readable — offered as "listen later" reminders), muted chats in WhatsApp send no notification so MEKA can't see them (setup screen explains the WhatsApp settings below), messages read first on another device may not notify.

Core `HereRules` (Here.kt, non-AI, pure; `HereTest`): `round` — a point leaves the device at two decimal places (`ROUND_DEG` 0.01°, about 1 km; NaN or off-Earth is null, so nothing odd is sent), `isRounded`, `distanceKm` (haversine), `isAway` (farther than `AWAY_KM` 15 km from every place already forecast: home and work), `asksAboutHere` ("here", "where I am / I'm", "near me", "around me", "nearby", "my location", "outside"; never "here's", "hear", "there", "where is"), `shouldLocate(on, permitted, question, lastFixMs, now)` (only with the switch on and the phone's permission; a question only when it is about here; Today's open at most once per `FIX_FRESH_MS` 30 min, the fix kept in memory), `nowLine` ("Near you · 12° · light rain from 16:00" for Today while away), `askLines` ("now where Meka is (approximate location): …", today, tomorrow), `setting(on, permitted)` (off by default "Off · the weather is home's and work's"; on without permission lit "Needs location (approximate is enough) · tap to allow"; the privacy words). Wire `HereCodec` (`HereCodecTest`): `POST /v1/weather/here` with `{lat, lon}` — the server **refuses** a point finer than two decimals rather than rounding it, so a precise location never reaches it; the answer is the mirrored weather's compact `hours`/`days` text plus `away` (the server knows home's and work's coordinates), or off/failed; oversized text dropped, never cut. Only `tools/core-verify.sh` could run here (957 tests green); one code push was left under today's cap, so this slice is the part that verification covers fully. Commit 1cfa3b3, CI run 37955206818 green (first try; that was today's 70th push, so the next code push waits for 00:00 UTC).

Wired from `integrations` by default in `mekaSync(here = …)`. Core `HereFix` (the rounded point, forecast, away, when) and `HereRules.fresh` / `todayLine` / `askLinesFor`. Facade: transport `HereApi.hereWeather` (404 → off, 403 → failed); `MekaCore.hereShouldLocate(on, permitted, question)`, `hereWeather(lat, lon)` (rounds with `HereRules.round` before anything is sent, NaN/off-Earth sends nothing; keeps the fix in memory only, 30 minutes), `forgetHere()`, `hereSetting(on, permitted)`; Today's `weatherView.nowLine` reads "Near you · 12° · …" while a fresh fix is away; Ask hears "now where Meka is (approximate location): …" only for a question about here. Tests: `HereTest.aFixIsUsedForHalfAnHour…`, backend `HereWeatherTest` (route keyed, finer point refused before Open-Meteo, failure, defaults), `HereFacadeTest`, `HttpSyncTransportTest.whereIAmNow…`.   - *Slice 1, 2026-10-10 (core + Fold + Mac; core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* the kit reminder. Core `FootballRules` (Football.kt, non-AI, pure; `FootballTest`, 8): a **club fixture** is an event of Meka's own calendars whose title or calendar names BUFC or SJFC as a whole word, any case (never the Barça fixtures feed, never an event still on its way to Google). **Kit reminder** on its detail (`EventActions.addKit`, `MekaCore.addKitReminder` → `KitAdded`) makes its kit task "Pack the kit for BUFC U9s v Arlesey" (id `k<event>`, linked to the event): planned at 19:00 the evening before for 10 min, Remind me at 19:00 (the task reminders' heads-up), due at kick-off (an all-day tournament: 09:00 on its first day); after 19:00 that evening just the list, due at kick-off. Its steps are **the kit list**: the steps of the most recent kit task that has any (so adding "Gloves" or removing "Coat" on one carries on to the next), else Boots · Shin pads · Kit and socks · Water bottle · Coat; laid down under ids from the task's (`Tasks.putChecklistItem`), so both devices offline make one list, and a deleted kit task added again comes back unticked. The detail's line: "Kit reminder tomorrow 19:00 · 2 of 5 packed" · "Kit list · 2 of 5 packed" · "Kit packed" (`EventDetailView.canKit` / `kitTaskId` / `kitLine`, `EventMarks.kitTasks`). **Fold:** the event detail's actions gain "Kit reminder" (light haptic; the undo bar rises with "Kit reminder tomorrow 19:00", Undo deletes it); the line joins the actions' note. **Mac:** the same button in the event sheet (`CoreModel.addKitReminder`, the event undo bar); bridge `testKitReminderReachesSwift`. Without the key the feed is `off` and nothing is sent. **Core** `TravelRules` (Travel.kt; non-AI, pure): Leave by = kick-off − 15 min early − the drive − 10 min to spare (`travelMin` = drive + 25); `FootballRules.leaveOffer` now offers **"Leave by 08:55 · 25 min drive"** first (rings as an alarm unless Meka turned it off at that ground last time), else the ground's "as last time"; the same one tap on both apps (`useLastLeaveBy`) sets it and the undo bar reads "Leave by 08:55 · 25 min drive · alarm". Setup's "Weather and travel" gains **Travel times** ("Add the Google key…" until the server has it); Calendars says "Travel times · off · add the Google key (see Setup)"; Health doesn't count an off feed as trouble. Tests: core `TravelTest` (5), SetupTest; backend `TravelTimesTest` (the request carries only coordinates and the place, parse, key in a header; no key sends nothing, first sight / evening before / hour before through the devices' offer, failure is the feed's error), infra test. Gradle can't run here; the backend test and LinesTest were compiled and run offline against the core with stubs for AWS. - [~] Done for the day (Meka approved 2026-10-09): an evening moment (from work end or 20:00): what got done (count-up), what carried over (one tap to tomorrow), tomorrow's first commitment and first train, weather; a satisfying close animation (the watch face dims to night). Builds on Shut down the day.
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-10 (core + tokens + Fold + Mac; core and macos touched, so Mac checked nightly):* everything but the first train. **Core** (EveningShutdown.kt): the evening now starts at work's end, else **20:00** (`DEFAULT_START_MIN`, was 18:00; an early finish or a night shift also waits for 20:00); `ShutdownView.done` (today's finished tasks, newest first, at most `DONE_SHOWN` 6, each with its time, `DoneRow`) and `doneMore` ("+ 2 more"); `firstLine` ("Tomorrow starts at 09:30 · Standup", the title cut like the glance's); `night` (shut down today). `WatchFaceRules.nightAlpha` (55 %) and `liveMode(…, night)` (a night face rests like the bedside's in quiet hours: no second hand or breath, redrawn each minute). New choreography token `nightFall` (1200 ms). **Fold:** the shutdown pane opens with "Done today" (a brass ✓, the title in body, the time) above Left from today, and the first commitment under Tomorrow's label; Today's watch face takes `night`, fading to 55 % over `nightFall` as the pane drops away. **Mac:** the same in ShutdownSheet (`DoneLine`), WatchFaceView/WatchFaceLiveLayer take `night`, TodayView fades the face. Carry-over (one tap, "Move the rest to tomorrow") and tomorrow's weather were already there. Tests: `EveningShutdownTest` (three new, two moved to 20:00), `BankHolidaysTest` (20:00), Mac bridge `testShutdownThroughTheBridge` (done rows, night, the night live mode). Motion catalogue's Evening shutdown row updated. Commit 3523e86: CI run 38040676574 green (first try), motion run 38040676582 green.
  - *What remains:* tomorrow's **first train** in the pane, which needs the trains half of "Places, location weather, per-day work hours and trains" (blocked, [!]); add it there when that lands. Otherwise done once CI is green.
  - *Check on the Fold:* on a Saturday the shutdown card appears at 20:00 (on a work day at work's end); open it: "Done today" lists what you ticked with the times, Tomorrow says what it starts with; Shut down: the check pops, the pane drops and the watch face fades to night, its second hand still. Next morning it is bright again.
- [~] Galaxy Watch, pulled forward from V2 (Meka approved 2026-10-09): Wear OS app: Up next with Done, train alerts, urgent call-assistant messages, start/stop a fast, quick capture by voice; a tile and a complication with the watch-face ring.
  - *Decided 2026-10-10:* the watch is a **device of its own** (its own Keystore key and its own copy of the day, synced with MEKA's server like the Fold and the Mac, over the watch's Wi-Fi/LTE or the phone's Bluetooth link), not a remote screen fed through Google's Wearable Data Layer, which may relay through Google's cloud when the two aren't on Bluetooth (new third-party data sharing, rule 5). It joins without the enrolment code: it shows an 8-digit code and Meka types it on the Fold or the Mac (ADR-005 amendment 2026-10-10).
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-10 (server + core + Fold + Mac; core and macos touched, so Mac checked nightly):* linking. **Server** (`DeviceLink`, Ktor-free; no migration): `POST /v1/link/start` (the watch, signed with its new key, no bearer → `{linkId, code, expiresAtMs}`, 10 minutes), `/v1/link/status` (signed with the same key → waiting · expired · linked with household, device id and secret, once), `/v1/devices/link` (a keyed device types the code → the watch is enrolled into its household with the key that asked; 5 wrong codes per 10 min per device; a code works once; a revoked watch id is refused), `/v1/devices/watches` and `/v1/devices/unlink` (watches only). Waiting links live in memory only (at most 10, at most 10 minutes). `DeviceRegistry.enrolLinked` / `linkedDevices` / `unlink` (Postgres and in-memory). **Core:** wire `DeviceLinkCodec` (every body, read strictly); domain `WatchLinkRules` (the code however it's typed, "1234 5678", rows "Linked today", the summary, refusals in words, the watch's own code screen for slice 2, a new watch id) and a new activity kind `DEVICE` ("Linked Galaxy Watch" / "Unlinked Galaxy Watch", once across devices); facade `DeviceLinkApi` on `HttpSyncTransport`, `MekaCore.watchLinkView` / `refreshWatches` / `linkWatch(code)` / `unlinkWatch(id)`, and `WatchLink.start` / `status` (the watch's half, signed with its key alone). **Fold:** Ask → More → Settings → **Watch** (connected devices only; `WatchPane`: summary, what to do on the watch, the code field with a number keyboard, Link, rows with Unlink). **Mac:** the same row and `WatchSheet` (`CoreModel.watchLink` / `linkWatch` / `unlinkWatch`; only Strings cross). Motion catalogue row "Watch". Tests: DeviceLinkCodecTest (3), WatchLinkTest (6), backend DeviceLinkTest (4, one through Ktor), PostgresDeviceLinkTest (CI's Postgres), WatchLinkFacadeTest (2), HttpSyncTransportTest (2 new), Fold ShellNavTest, Mac ShellNavTests + bridge `testWatchLinkReachesSwift`. Commit 7f2d3ba: CI run 310 (38042069801) green (first try); the deploy follows (new server routes). *Check on the Fold:* Ask → More → Watch shows "No watch linked yet" and the code field (nothing to link until slice 2's watch app).
  - *Slice 2, 2026-10-10 (core + Fold build + new watch app; core touched, so Mac checked nightly):* the Wear OS app. **Core:** `WatchHomeRules` (WatchHome.kt, non-AI, pure; `WatchHomeTest`, 6): the watch's one screen from the cover screen's now card, keeping only what a wrist can do in one tap (Done · Tomorrow on a task, Went · Didn't go on a booked session; Join, Directions and Open stay on the phone), "Then: …", and the fast (running: title, "14:05", the goal or day line, the ring's share, End fast; resting: the eating window's line, Start fast); the watch's own lines (unlinked, no server, offline). Facade `MekaCore.watchHome()`, `watchPress(button)` (the same changes as the cover screen's taps) and `watchFastButton()` (MekaCoreTest `theWatchShowsUpNextTakesDoneAndTomorrowAndStartsAndEndsAFastForTheFold`: a watch core's Done, Tomorrow and fast reach the Fold through the server). **Watch app** (`:wear:app`, plain Compose from the phone's BOM, no Material or Wear libraries; same application id `os.meka.android`; standalone, so it needs the phone only as its network): first open makes its Keystore key (`WatchDeviceKey`) and device id, asks MEKA's server for a code (`WatchLink.start`) and shows it (`WatchLinkRules.codeScreen`; kept, so reopening within 10 minutes shows the same code), checks every 3 s while on screen (`WatchLink.status`); once linked it opens its own SQLCipher replica (`DatabaseKeyStore`) and runs `MekaCore`, syncing while on screen (`LifecycleStartEffect`). The day: the now card, the fast with its ring, an offline line; the crown scrolls. Unlinked from the Fold or the Mac (sync signed out): "This watch was unlinked · Link again" deletes the replica, identity and key and starts over. Server address built in by `tools/install-watch.sh` (asks once, kept in `~/.meka/server-url` on the Mac; pairs the watch over Wi-Fi with an `ip:port`). Tokens: `design/generate.mjs` writes the same MekaTokens into the watch's package; type-check and rhythm-check cover the watch's sources; `tools/make-app-icon.py` writes the watch's icon too. CI: the android job builds and lints `:wear:app`. ADR-005 note; motion catalogue row "Galaxy Watch (the watch app)". Gradle can't run in the container, so the watch build is proven by CI. Commit 1a87d0d: CI run 311 built and linted the watch app first time, and the new facade test caught a real fault from slice 1: a watch's id `watch-…` is the HLC's node id on every replica, which refuses a "-", so the watch's core couldn't have started. Fix: ids are `watch` and 16 hex digits (`WatchLinkRules.ID_PREFIX`, `DeviceLinkCodec.isWatchId` on the server, and the Postgres registry's watch list, which matched `watch-%`, now `watch%` filtered by the same rule; no watch had linked, so nothing stored changes; ADR-005 updated). Commits aec7e6a (CI run 312: everything green but PostgresDeviceLinkTest, which caught the list's `LIKE`) and 33511c7: CI run 313 (38044104079) green, the watch app built and linted; the deploy follows (deploy run 38044203043, the server's watch-id rule). *Check on the watch:* Needs Meka #20.
  - *Slice 3, 2026-10-10 (core + watch app; core touched, so Mac checked nightly; no Mac or Fold change):* the tile and the complication. **Core** (WatchTile.kt, non-AI, pure; `WatchTileTest`, 8): `WatchTileRules.tile` — the watch's "now" card shortened for the tile: the label, the title cut at a word to 40 characters, its line, **one button** (the screen's primary one: Done on a task, Went on a booked session) and a running fast's "Fasting · goal 16 h · 14:05" (success colour once reached), with one spoken line; unlinked: "LINK THIS WATCH · Open MEKA". A press comes back as the tile's last clickable id (`pressId` "press:DONE:<task>"), and `pressed` answers only a button the watch offers now, so a tile drawn before the task was done on the phone does nothing. `complication` — MEKA's ring as a ranged value: a running fast's share of its goal with its clock ("Fast", "Goal" once reached), else today's list done ("2/5", "Clear" when there was nothing), at most 7 characters, spoken "2 of 5 done today"; `refreshAfterMs` (the next minute while a fast or an "In 12 min" counts, else 15 minutes). Facade `watchTile`, `watchTilePress(id)`, `watchComplication`, `watchRefreshAfterMs` (MekaCoreTest `theWatchTilesDoneReachesTheFoldAndAStaleTileDoesNothing`). **Watch app** (wear/…/surfaces): `WatchTileService` (androidx.wear.tiles 1.5 / protolayout 1.3: the card in MekaType sizes and MEKA's dark colours, the brass Done pill reloading the tile with its id, a tap elsewhere opening MEKA, a content description for the screen reader), `WatchComplicationService` (watchface-complications-data-source-ktx 1.2.1: RANGED_VALUE and SHORT_TEXT, a tap opens MEKA, a 5-minute update period), `WatchSyncWorker` (WorkManager, every 15 minutes once linked, cancelled on unlink) and `WatchSurfaces.refresh` (after each background sync and a second after Today or the fast changes on the watch). New libraries' versions are pinned from what was known to be published; the container can't reach Google's Maven, so CI is their first check. ADR-007 addendum; motion catalogue row "Galaxy Watch tile and complication". Commit 7c54112 (CI run 314 compiled everything, and lint refused protolayout's experimental `FONT_WEIGHT_MEDIUM`), fixes fa7442c and c85cdf5 (the tile uses bold from 600, else normal): CI run 316 (38046189290) green; the core was touched, so the server redeploys. *Check on the watch (after `./tools/install-watch.sh`):* swipe left from the watch face → + → MEKA · Up next: the tile shows Up next with Done; tap Done: it shows what's next and the Fold has it done. Long-press the watch face → Edit → a complication slot → MEKA · Fast or today: the ring shows "2/5" (or a fast's clock).
  - *Slice 4a, 2026-10-10 (core + watch app; core touched, so Mac checked nightly; no Fold or Mac change):* quick capture by voice. **Core** (WatchCapture.kt, non-AI, pure; `WatchCaptureTest`, 5): `WatchCaptureRules.clean` (one line, spaces collapsed, first letter capitalised, a lone full stop the recogniser added dropped, at most 200 characters cut at a word; null for silence), `captured` ("Added “…”" with the title cut at a word to 32, or the quick alarm's own line), `failLine` per `WatchListenFailure` (no on-device recogniser · no English speech · no microphone · not heard · failed), Undo for 5 s; `WatchTileView.capture` and `WatchTileRules.CAPTURE_ID`. Facade `MekaCore.watchCapture(heard)` (as if typed into Today's capture bar: `captureTyped`, so "timer 20 min" sets a timer) and `watchCaptureUndo` (deletes the task or cancels the alarm everywhere) — MekaCoreTest `aWatchCaptureByVoiceReachesTheFoldAndUndoTakesItBack`. **Watch app:** `WatchListener` (the watch's **on-device** recogniser only, as the Fold's Talk; English fetched on Android 13+ when missing), Capture under Up next on the day screen (`CaptureBlock`: Listening… with the words as heard, then the line with Undo; RECORD_AUDIO asked on the first tap; package visibility for the recogniser), and the tile's Capture beside Done, which opens MEKA already listening (`WatchCaptureRules.LISTEN_EXTRA`). ADR-006 note; motion catalogue row "Galaxy Watch capture by voice". Commit 469bc2c (CI run 317: core and the facade test green; the watch's tile had a leftover second Capture that didn't compile) and fix 135a8d0: CI run 318 (38047253963) green, the watch app built and linted; the core was touched, so the server redeploys.
  - *Check on the watch (after `./tools/install-watch.sh`):* open MEKA → Capture → allow the microphone → say "buy milk": "Added “Buy milk”" with Undo, and the Fold's Today has it. Say "timer 10 minutes": the timer is set (it rings on the Fold). From the tile, tap Capture: MEKA opens listening. If the watch says it can't listen on its own, it has no on-device recogniser: say so, and capture stays on the phone.
  - *What remains:* slice 4b, MEKA's own on-watch alerts — train alerts and urgent call-assistant messages already reach the watch as the phone's notifications (Wear OS bridges them), so first check on the watch whether that is enough (the Fold's MEKA notifications must not be marked local-only); only build watch-side alerts if they don't arrive or need the watch's own actions. *Code check, 2026-10-10:* no notification on the Fold is marked local-only (`setLocalOnly` appears nowhere in the app), so Wear OS bridges MEKA's train alerts and urgent call-assistant messages to the watch with their action buttons; nothing buildable until Meka has looked on the watch (after Needs Meka #20: say whether they arrive).
