Enrolling a brand-new device id is still open to whoever holds the code: that closes with passkey enrolment or "approve this new device on your Fold" (V1, with the Recovery key item); until then rotating the code after setting up a device shuts it.
  - *Seen 2026-10-06 (run 55):* `PostgresOpStoreTest.concurrentPushesGetGapFreeSequencesAndNoDuplicates` failed once with a Hikari connection timeout (`SQLTransientConnectionException` at the retry loop; Postgres logged "unexpected EOF on client connection with an open transaction") and passed on re-run; the commit didn't touch the backend. If it recurs, look for a connection held across the concurrent duplicate pushes in `PostgresOpStore` (pool of 8, 400 concurrent pushes).
  - *Fixed 2026-10-07 (recurred in run 37659606955):* `PostgresOpStore.conn` was `current.get()?.let(block) ?: ds.connection.use(block)`, so a block returning null (finding an op not stored yet) ran again on a second pooled connection while the push's transaction held its own; eight concurrent pushes then starved the pool of eight until Hikari's 30 s timeout. Now it uses the transaction's connection or a fresh one, never both (also halves the queries for every new op the server stores).

### V1 · The assistant layer
- [x] MEKA as the phone's default assistant (side-button long-press opens Ask) + voice: "move my dentist to Friday", "what did the school say?", "remind me to call Mum when I leave work".
  - *Talk to MEKA — conversation mode (Meka, 2026-10-08 12:55; take as the first V1 item once the AI layer's first slice lands):* a back-and-forth spoken conversation, not one-shot commands. Listen: on-device speech recognition (Android `SpeechRecognizer` offline English; Mac `SFSpeechRecognizer` on-device), nothing recorded or stored as audio. Understand and act: the AI layer with MEKA's own tools only (create/move/complete tasks, add/move calendar events, read Today/Needs you/brief/news/fasting, start a fast, set an alarm/timer) — every change goes through the policy engine at the same levels as tapping; anything that sends a message or email is drafted and needs a spoken or tapped "send it"; destructive changes ("delete…") confirm first. Speak: on-device text-to-speech (Android `TextToSpeech` with the best installed Google voice; Mac `AVSpeechSynthesizer` premium voices), short answers ("Added 'Call the dentist' for tomorrow at 9. Anything else?"), with an undo chip on screen for every change. Turn-taking: keeps listening after it answers until silence/“that's all”; barge-in (talking over it stops it). Starts from: a mic button in Ask and on Today's capture bar, the side-button long-press (default assistant), a home-screen widget, the bedside clock, and Bluetooth headset button; the Mac with ⌥Space. Read-outs: "read my brief", "what's the Barça news?", "read the headlines" (headlines read as plain text, untrusted — never acted on). No always-on wake word in the first version (battery, false triggers); revisit later. Motion: a voice orb that breathes while idle, swells with your voice level while listening, ripples while MEKA speaks, and the live transcript types in under it; reduced motion: a static ring with a level bar. Cost: speech in/out is free on device; each turn is one AI call (pennies), inside the $40 cap; show a daily count in Activity. A premium neural voice is a later option (needs Meka's OK on cost).
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-08 (core touched, so Mac checked nightly; no app screen yet):* **the conversation**, core and server. **Core** (core/domain/Talk.kt, non-AI, pure): `TalkRules.reply` reads a final transcript: a goodbye ("that's all", "no thanks, bye", "ok cheers"; "ok" or "great" alone isn't one) ends; with cards on offer a yes ("yes please", "go ahead", "ok") confirms the one card, "the second one" / "the last one" / "the first and the third" / "both" (two only) / "all of them" pick, a bare yes with several makes MEKA ask which, a no ("no, leave it", "not now") leaves them on screen; anything else is a new question. What MEKA says: `spoken` (the answer made speakable: no list marks, markdown, quotes or emoji, "09:00–09:15" → "09:00 to 09:15", at most three sentences / 320 characters; then "Shall I move Book dentist to tomorrow at 09:00?" or "I can set a timer for 20 minutes or start a 36-hour fast. Say which, or both."), `spokenDone` ("Moved Book dentist to tomorrow at 09:00. Anything else?"), `spokenWhich`, `LEFT_IT`. `TalkFlow` is the turn-taking the apps will run: listen → ask → speak → listen again; silence or a goodbye ends quietly; talking over MEKA stops it and listens (barge-in); AI unavailable is said once and ends; twelve questions per conversation, then "That's a lot for one go. Tap the mic to carry on."; late events after a stop change nothing. `Conversation` keeps the exchanges in memory only (the last six are sent, with up to three confirmed lines each). **Wire:** `AskCodec.Request.history` (`[{q, a, done?}]`, at most six; limits refused) and `voice`, both left out of the JSON for a one-off question so older servers see the same request. **Server** (AiAsk.kt): history becomes alternating user/assistant turns (earlier answers as words only, so a card is only ever offered from the latest answer), what Meka confirmed leads the next question ("Since then Meka did: …"), the `<today>` block only in the latest turn; `voice` adds a line asking for one or two short sentences that read well aloud. **Facade:** `MekaCore.talk(question, history)` (one call, never throws, cards checked and policy-filtered as in `askMeka`); a spoken yes runs the cards through `doAsk` like a tap, so each change keeps its undo chip. If MEKA ever cuts itself off without you talking, or never stops for you on the speaker, say which (and roughly how loud the phone was): the numbers are the core's `BargeInRules.SPEAKER` and a run can tune them. Ask → More → Talk → Talk over MEKA → Turn off puts it back to tapping only.
  - *Later, if wanted:* keep the first words (hand the level reader's last half-second to the recogniser) — Android's on-device recogniser can't take audio from an app, so this would be Mac-only; not built.

- [~] "What MEKA did and why" activity log with undo (lands before any automatic action)
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-07 (Mac checked nightly):* core `ActivityLog` (non-AI), unit-tested incl. two-device sync, over a new `agent_action` entity (additive, all LWW; ADR-006/008 addenda). What it records today: every notification that actually reached you at Critical, Needs a decision or Heads-up ("Reminded you: Cancel or keep Netflix?" · "Cancel by tomorrow" · "Why: Cancel-by dates · Heads-up"), and every digest that went out ("Sent a digest: Evening digest · 3 things"); one entry per notice however many devices posted it. The undo machinery is in place for the automatic actions to come: `ActivityLog.act` is the one way an automatic action may change Meka's data, recording each field's value before; `undo` puts back only what is still as MEKA left it ("Partly undone: you've changed some of it since"), deletes rather than blanks something MEKA created, and converges when undone on both devices offline. The screen: the last 30 days by day, newest first, "This week: 5 reminders · 2 digests", time, what, the detail, why, and Undo or "Undone at 09:12". Interruption counts stay text-free (ADR-013); the log lives in Meka's own synced data and goes into the export. Fold: "Activity" in Today's header opens the pane; the governor now reports the digest when it reached a live channel. Mac: "Activity" in Today, File → Activity… (⇧⌘A), a sheet; MacNotifier reports the digest by its key (`meka.digest`). CI green (run 76; macOS job at the next nightly run; the core was touched, so the server redeploys).
  - *Remaining:* nothing automatic exists yet, so no entry can be undone in the apps today; each automatic action (V1 policy engine onwards) must go through `ActivityLog.act`. Later slices: "Prevented misses" (ADR-013) from reminders followed by the item being done before it was due; filtering by kind; the Fold's work-mode alerts and after-work nudge in the log once they move onto the governor.

First slice: the service reads the key (empty = AI off), one health check call, and a server-side reminder that the key expires on 2027-10-07 (shown in Needs you from 2027-09-20). *(Landed 2026-10-08 with the Outlook item: the AI key is on the renewals radar as "Renew MEKA's AI key", in Needs you from 2027-09-20.)* Ask in words should cover starting a fast ("start a 5-day fast", "fast until Friday 6pm") through the existing `startExtendedFast`/`startFastUntil`.
  - *Slice 1, 2026-10-08 (server only, no app change):* the service reads its key and checks it. `AnthropicKey.parse` reads `{"api_key"}` from `meka-os-dev/ai/anthropic` (env `MEKA_AI_SECRET`, already granted read-only to the service): `{}`, blank or anything that isn't an `sk-ant-` key means **AI off**; read at use time (cached a minute), so pasting or rotating the key needs no redeploy. `AiHealth` (backend/Ai.kt): the health check is Anthropic's free `GET /v1/models?limit=1` (no prompt, none of Meka's data, no tokens spent), trusted for six hours when it answers, re-checked after ten minutes when it fails ("Anthropic refused the key", "…limiting requests (spend cap or rate limit)", "Couldn't reach Anthropic"), and at once when the key changes. `POST /v1/ai/status` (keyed devices only; the release publisher refused) answers `{"state": "on"|"off"|"failing", "checkedAtMs", "reason"}`, never the key; the server logs only `ai: on` / `ai: failing (…)` about 20 s after it starts. The key's renewal reminder landed earlier (Outlook item). Tests: AiHealthTest (parsing, off without a call, the check's headers, caching, failures, key change, route auth, route left out without the secret). ADR-006 addendum. CI green (run 37811348211; the server redeploys). **Next:** the `LanguageModelProvider` on the server (Anthropic messages, a token meter and the monthly budget with a soft alert at 70 % and off at 100 %, ADR-006 §5), then Ask MEKA through the core (status shown in Ask on both apps), then Talk to MEKA (the default-assistant item above).
  - *Slice 2, 2026-10-08 (server only, no app change):* the cloud `LanguageModelProvider`, its token meter and the monthly budget (ADR-006 §4–5). backend/AiModels.kt: `ModelRequest` (a feature name for the meter, a tier, a system line and the turns; output clamped to 4,096 tokens), `ModelOutcome` (Answered · Off · OverBudget · Failed with the reason in words; everything but Answered means "carry on without AI"). `AnthropicProvider` calls the Messages API with the key read at use time and **no tools** (text in, text out); `ModelCatalog` picks each tier's newest model from Anthropic's model list once a day (SMALL = newest Haiku, FRONTIER = newest Sonnet; `claude-haiku-4-5` / `claude-sonnet-4-5` if the list fails). The meter (`ai_usage`, migration V10, additive: per UTC month and feature, calls, tokens in/out and list price in micro-dollars; counts only, nothing of Meka's) prices Haiku $1/$5, Sonnet $3/$15, Opus $5/$25 per million tokens (cache tokens counted as input; an unknown model at the highest price). `AiBudget`: MEKA's share of the $40 Anthropic cap is **$20 a month** (`MEKA_AI_BUDGET_USD` changes it, no redeploy of code); from 70 % the status says `alert` and the log says so once; at 100 % calls are refused before reaching Anthropic until the 1st (the Console cap stays the hard stop). `POST /v1/ai/status` now carries `budget: {month, spentCents, budgetCents, level, features[]}`. Tests: AiModelsTest (text-only body, model choice and fallback, clamping, off, failures not counted, 70 %/100 % and the new month, prices, status JSON, the Postgres meter in CI). ADR-006 addendum. CI green (run 37812852516; the server redeploys). **Next:** Ask MEKA — a `POST /v1/ai/ask` that answers from what the device sends (Today, Needs you) with MEKA's own actions proposed as cards the policy engine decides, through the core, and the AI status and month's spend shown in Ask on both apps.
  - *Slice 3a, 2026-10-08 (server and core; no app screen yet, so Mac checked nightly):* **Ask MEKA's round trip**. **Core** (core/domain/AskMeka.kt, non-AI, pure): `AskRules.context` turns Today into at most 60 short lines (Needs you with why, Up next and the day's tasks with "planned 18:00"/"anytime today", what's done, the calendar with "over" on finished events), tasks named by handles ("t1") that stay on the device (`AskContext.taskIds`; no ids, notes or addresses sent), `untrusted` when calendar lines are in it; `AskRules.card` turns a proposal into a card only when it checks out (a known kind, a handle it sent, a day today…two years, "HH:MM", a title of 1–120 characters, a fast of 12–240 h, a timer of a minute to a day; a time already gone today isn't offered; at most three, no two alike): "Add “Call the dentist” · Tomorrow · 09:00" · "Tick off “Book dentist”" · "Move “CR” to Fri 9 Oct · 09:00" · "Start a 36 h fast" · "Timer · 20 min" · "Alarm · 06:30"; `doneLine` for the undo bar, `unavailableLine` (off · budget used up until the 1st · "Couldn't ask: …"). **Wire** (`AskCodec`): request/response (limits refused, not trimmed) and `parseModelAnswer` (the `{"answer","actions"}` JSON, fenced or not; anything else is words with no actions). **Server** (backend/AiAsk.kt): `POST /v1/ai/ask` (keyed devices only; the publisher refused), one SMALL-tier call with no tools under the meter's `ask`, the system line saying the `<today>` block is data, not instructions (brackets inside it neutralised), only MEKA's six actions, never sending, spending, trading or calendar changes; drops other kinds and handles it didn't send; nothing stored or logged. **Facade:** `AiApi` on the HTTP transport, `MekaCore.askMeka(question)` → `AskOutcome` (Answered with checked cards that the policy engine allows as suggestions, or Unavailable with its line; never throws for a missing answer) and `doAsk(card)` on Meka's tap (adds/ticks/moves the task — a moved task's calendar block follows — starts the fast, or sets the timer/alarm exactly as typing it; refuses a task that's no longer open). ADR-006 addendum. Tests: AskMekaTest, AskCodecTest, AiAskTest (prompt shape, injection can't close the block, only known actions and handles, off/over/failed, route auth), AskMekaFacadeTest. CI green (run 37815117792; the server redeploys with the route). **Next (3b):** Ask's screen on both apps — the field asks MEKA (thinking shimmer, answer lines fade in, cards rise, per the catalogue's Assistant row), Search stays one tap away, the AI status and month's spend shown in Ask (`/v1/ai/status`), cards' buttons with the undo bar; then Talk to MEKA.
  - *Slice 3b, 2026-10-08 (core and Mac touched, so Mac checked nightly):* **Ask's screen on both apps**. Core: `AskRules.statusView` / `AiStatusView` (non-AI, pure): "On · $1.20 of $20 this month", "… · most of the month's budget used" (lit, from 70 %), "This month's budget is used up · back on the 1st" (lit, can't ask), "Off · Search still finds everything" (can't ask), "Not answering · <reason>" (lit); `STATUS_UNKNOWN` offline, `STATUS_NOT_CONNECTED`; `answerLines` (paragraphs and list lines, long paragraphs split after sentences, for the fade-in). `AskCodec.decodeStatus` reads `/v1/ai/status` (state, reason, the budget's cents and level). `AiApi.aiStatus()` on the HTTP transport (404 = off), `MekaCore.aiStatus()` never throws. Every card can be taken back: `doAsk` now returns `AskDone(line, undo)` and `MekaCore.undoAsk(undo)` removes the task it added, puts a ticked-off or moved task back (`Tasks.putBack`, only while it is still as the card left it; a change since wins), throws away the fast while it runs (`Fasting.discardIfOpen`) or cancels the timer/alarm. Tests: AskMekaTest (status lines, dollars, answer lines), AskCodecTest (status), AskMekaFacadeTest (undo for every kind, changed since, status from the server/offline/not connected), Mac bridge test. **Fold** (ask/AskMekaSection.kt): Ask's field is a text field ("Ask MEKA…", Send on the keyboard) with Ask (accent once there's a question) and Search pills beside it; while asking can't work the field opens Search as before; the status line under it; the question, a shimmer, the answer's lines fading in, cards rising with their button; a tapped card folds away and Ask's undo bar rises ("Added “Milk” · Undo"); a polite live region for screen readers. **Mac** (Ask/AskMekaSection.swift): the same in Ask (Return asks, Search ⌘F beside it), the shell's undo bar, Undo brings the card back. No new fields, no sync change, no server change. (1) Sign up at twilio.com and upgrade the account (add a card; Twilio requires an address for UK numbers — the "regulatory bundle"). (2) Phone Numbers → Buy a number → United Kingdom, with Voice. (3) On the number's page, Voice configuration → "A call comes in" → Webhook, HTTP POST, `<your SyncUrl>/v1/voice/twilio/incoming` (SyncUrl is the stack output, `https://….cloudfront.net`). (4) Account → Account info: copy the Account SID and Auth token into Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/voice/twilio` as `{"account_sid": "AC…", "auth_token": "…"}` (no redeploy needed). (5) Settings → General → turn on "Enforce HTTP Auth on Media URLs" so recordings aren't public while they wait to be transcribed. (6) On the Fold: Phone → ⋮ → Settings → Supplementary services → Call forwarding → Voice call → "Forward when busy" → the Twilio number. (7) In MEKA's Work screen, turn on Call assistant and "Let MEKA screen calls". Then call the Fold from another phone during work hours to try it.
14. **Phone signing key for GitHub** — done 2026-10-07 (`MEKA_FOLD_SIGNING_KEY` is set; GitHub published its first phone build the same night). If the Mac's debug key ever changes, re-run `tools/Copy phone signing key.command`.
17. **Google OAuth to Production** — while MEKA's Google app is in Testing, Google ends every sign-in after 7 days (MEKA now warns a day ahead on Today with Reconnect, 2026-10-09). To stop it: Google Cloud console → Google Auth Platform (or APIs & Services → OAuth consent screen) → Audience → **Publish app** → Confirm. Personal use needs no verification; Google shows an "unverified app" warning on the next sign-in (Advanced → Go to MEKA). Then reconnect Google once in Calendars so the new sign-in is a Production one.
16. **Train data key (National Rail)** — for live train times: steps to follow when the trains item is ready (free account on National Rail's data service, copy the key into Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/rail/darwin`).
15. **Mac desktop News widget** (2026-10-08): after the next `tools/install-mac.sh`, right-click the desktop → Edit Widgets… and look for "MEKA News". If it isn't listed (macOS may refuse widgets from a build not signed through the Apple Developer Program), say so: the next step would be the Developer Program (#6, skipped) or dropping the Mac widget; the floating ticker covers the same need meanwhile.
16. **AI budget** (2026-10-08, no action needed unless you disagree): MEKA keeps its AI spend to **$20 a month**, half the $40 Anthropic cap it shares with Kestrel; it warns at 70 % and switches AI off at 100 % until the 1st. To change it, say the number and a run sets `MEKA_AI_BUDGET_USD`.
18. **Family calendar for Jeanette** (2026-10-10, family sharing slice 3): which of your events should Jeanette see on her page, and where should events she adds go? Suggested: she sees only the events on one calendar you pick (say a "Family" Google calendar; none of your others), and what she adds goes into that same calendar through MEKA's calendar editing, marked "From Jeanette". The other way is a MEKA-only family calendar that never touches Google. Reply with your choice (and the calendar's name); until then that slice waits and the rest of the board carries on.
19. **Travel time to football (weekend football, slice 2)** (2026-10-10): a leave-by alarm "with travel time from home" needs a routing service to work the drive out (home and the ground's address are sent to it). Free options: OpenRouteService (free key, data to HeiGIT in Germany) or Google's Routes API (a key on a Google Cloud billing account; free at this volume). Reply with "ORS", "Google" or "no provider"; until then MEKA remembers the travel time you set for each ground and offers it next time. **Answered 2026-10-10 07:48: Google** (Routes API, traffic-aware, Pro SKU: 5,000 free a month; expected use ~50–150 lookups a month, so £0). Build it now with the key read from Secrets Manager `meka-os-dev/maps/google` (`{"api_key":"…"}`), server-side only (never on a device); until the secret exists the feature stays off and Setup shows "Travel times · add the Google key" with the steps. Send home and the ground's place only (no names, no event text). Look up when the fixture is added, the evening before, and ~1 h before leave-by (traffic). Setup steps for Meka (give him these when the slice lands): console.cloud.google.com → new project "MEKA" → Billing: link a billing account (he adds the card himself) → APIs & Services → enable **Routes API** → Credentials → Create API key → restrict it to Routes API only → Quotas: cap Compute Routes at **100 requests/day** → AWS console (eu-west-2) → Secrets Manager → `meka-os-dev/maps/google` (MEKA made it, empty) → Retrieve secret value → Edit → Plaintext `{"api_key":"AIza…"}` → Save (no redeploy needed; MEKA reads it within a few minutes). **Built 2026-10-10 (slice 2b): only the key is left;** until then Setup shows "Travel times · Add the Google key". Also set a £1 budget alert in Billing → Budgets.
20. **Install MEKA on the Galaxy Watch** (2026-10-10, Galaxy Watch slice 2): on the watch, Settings → About watch → Software → tap "Software version" 5 times; then Developer options → turn on **ADB debugging** and **Wireless debugging** (same Wi-Fi as the Mac) → **Pair new device**. On the Mac, from the repo: `./tools/install-watch.sh <ip:port shown on the watch>` and type the 6-digit pairing code; it asks once for MEKA's server address (the one the Fold was connected with). MEKA opens on the watch with an 8-digit code: on the Fold, Ask → More → Watch, type it, Link. The watch then shows Up next with Done and Tomorrow and the fast with Start/End; tap Done on the watch and the Fold shows it done within seconds. Later installs: `./tools/install-watch.sh` with no address.
21. **Mac screenshots for the Mac review** (2026-10-10): when you're at the Mac, take screenshots (⇧⌘4, then Space, click the window) of Today, Needs you, Calendar, Lists, Ask and More, and one sheet you use often (e.g. a task's detail), in Dark, plus Today in Light, and share them in a build chat. The Mac gets the same polish pass the Fold had from them; nothing else is needed.

Otherwise type the days off once in Ask → More → School ("Half term 26–30 Oct", "INSET 4 Jan") and nothing else is needed.
